Search plan in `propose_candidate(budget_s=1800)`:
- Set deadline = now + budget - 5.
- Initialize best sequence and best_score = evaluate on height_sequence_1 maybe (if available) or two-block construction length e.g., 256 or 512? Starting from parent may be score 2 but we can get 1.777 with two-block quickly.
- Define helper functions top-level:
  - `eval_list(seq)`: robust, call evaluate_sequence, return inf on exception.
  - `convolve_max(p)`: FFT returns c array and max. For length up to maybe 4096 use rfft. For very small maybe np.convolve exact. Need ensure real and nonnegative; clip tiny negatives. Compute coef = ifft(f*f, n=L)[:2n-1]. Use `np.fft.rfft`? `np.fft.irfft(f * f.conj(), n=L)`? For real p, `f=np.fft.rfft(p, L); coeff=np.fft.irfft(f*f, L)[:2n-1]`; since square in complex -> real. Correct for linear convolution. Use next power of 2? n up to 4096; choose L=1; while L<2n-1: L<<=1. For many evals, precompute L? But p length fixed; helper can cache? Top-level with global dict? Use global variables? We can store in function attributes? Top-level no closures but can have global variables. Simpler: compute L inside; overhead small. For speed, use `L = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()`. Real FFT length maybe power of 2.
  - `score_from_p(p)`: compute `2*n*max(c)/(p.sum()**2)`. But p normalized sum1; if p sum not 1, rescale. For our search, maintain sum1. Return F.
  - `normalize(p)`: p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 p=ones; return p/s.
  - `two_block_sequence(n, ratio=2/3, reverse=False)`: first half or length? If n odd, split m=n//2. Use masses ratio for first block. Return p: first m values = ratio/m? If total mass P=ratio in first m, height P/m; second mass 1-P height (1-P)/(n-m). This is constant within block. Objective approx two-block; for exact maybe 1.777. If n even. Could also use P=0.5? no. Use P=2/3? For half blocks, optimum 2/3,1/3. If n not power of 2, still.
  - `random_sequence(n, rng)`: e.g., Dirichlet(random gamma). Also maybe add Fourier modes.
  - `fourier_sequence(n, A, mode)`: p_i = 1 + A cos(π i/(n-1))? But constant term? Need nonnegative. Use anti-sym mode: `p = 1 + A * cos(np.pi * i/(n-1))` or sin? For A up to ? To have nonnegative, A≤1 maybe. Also use mixture of modes: `p = exp(sum A_m cos(mπx))`? Generate.
- Local optimizer `optimize_sequence(p, rng, deadline, min_time=...)`:
  Maintains p normalized. At each iteration:
    c, max = convolve_max(p)
    score = 2n max
    update best.
    Choose weights w:

Let's design code structure:
```
import numpy as np
import time

_PARAMS = {"deadline":0.0, "rng":None}

def _now(): return time.monotonic()

def _get_fft_len(n):
    m=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L<m: L <<=1
    return L

def _fft_conv(p):
    n=len(p)
    L=_get_fft_len(n)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, L)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return c

def _max_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    n=p.size
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf
    p=p/s
    c=_fft_conv(p)
    m=c.max() if c.size else 0
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```

We need h_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_j w_{i+j}. This is cross-correlation with w length m=2n-1. Use FFT length L ≥ n+m-1 (3n-2). Let P = rfft(p, L). Let R = rfft(rev_w, L), where rev_w[t] = w[L-1-t]? Linear convolution C = A * rev(B) where rev(B) length m with revB[t]=B[m-1-t]. C[k] = ∑_j A_j revB[k-j] = ∑ A_j B[m-1-(k-j)] = B[j + (m-1-k)]. We want B[j+i]. Set m-1-k = i => k=m-1-i. Thus h_i = C[m-1-i]. Great. So define `revB = np.zeros(L); revB[0:m] = w[::-1]`. Then C=irfft(fft(A)*fft(revB)). h = C[m-1 - i] for i=0..n-1. Need L ≥ n+m-1 to avoid circular. m=2n-1, L≥3n-2. Good.
```
def _make_h(p,w):
   n=len(p); m=len(w)
   L=1 << (n+m-1).bit_length()
   f=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   rev=np.zeros(L); rev[:m]=w[::-1]
   g=np.fft.rfft(rev,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(f*g, L)
   h=c[m-1 - np.arange(n)]
   return h
```
Check for i=0: index m-1; C[m-1] = ∑ A_j B[j]? Good.
For i=n-1: index m-n = n-1; C[n-1] = ∑ A_j B[j+n-1] valid up to j<n; B index up to 2n-2=m-1; good.

Mirror update:
```
def _mirror_step(p, w, gamma):
    h = _make_h(p,w)
    hc = h - np.mean(h)
    z = -gamma * hc
    z = np.clip(z, -8, 8)
    p2 = p * np.exp(z)
    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-12)
    p2 /= p2.sum()
    return p2
```
But if w is not normalized? make sum1.

Weights from c:
```
def _smooth_weights(c, power):
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    w=c**power
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w=np.ones(len(c))
    return w/s
```
Could also add uniform: `w = (1-mix)*smooth + mix*uniform`, then normalize. Mixing uniform may prevent depending only on peaks.

```
def _block_perturb(p, rng):
    q=p.copy()
    width = int(np.random.randint(max(1,n//50), n//5+1))
    pos=random
    q[pos:pos+width] *= rng.normal(1,0.1, width)? ensure positive.
    # maybe sym? q=0.5(q+q[::-1]) reduces but symmetric bad; skip.
    return normalize(q)
```

```
def _lp_improve(p, deadline, max_it=10, step=0.25):
    n=len(p)
    if n>700: maybe skip? Dense 2n-1 x n. For n=512, 1023*512~523k. For n=1024, ~2M, okay but solving may be slow. Limit n<=768.
    M = _fft_max(p)/(p.sum()?) Actually p sum1, M_abs = max(c). Constraint conv(p,q) <= M_abs.
    for it in range(max_it):
       if _now()>deadline: break
       build A ...
       res=linprog(-ones, A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
       if not res.success: break
       q=res.x
       qsum=q.sum()
       if qsum<=0: break
       q=q/qsum
       best_p=p; best_sc=score(p)
       for t in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5]:
           p2=normalize((1-t)*best_p + t*q)
           sc=score(p2)
           if sc<best_sc: accept; break
       if no improve: break? Maybe still accept best q? If p2 all worse, maybe try q with small t? Use line search.
```

Pseudo:
```
gamma_factor = 1.0
fail=0
for it:
  c,Cmax,...,w, P/h
  score=2*Cmax
  if score<best: best
  # update
  scale = max(std(h), mean(h)*0.01, 1e-12)
  gamma = 0.5*gamma_factor/scale
  p1 = mirror(p,h,gamma)
  sc1 = score_fft(p1)
  if sc1 < score: p=p1; gamma_factor*=1.01? fail=0
  else:
     # try p2 with product
     hp=p*h; mean=np.dot(p,hp); scale2=...; p2=mirror(p,hp - mean? actually use hprod centered)

        # random block perturb

        if sc3<score: p=p3
        else: gamma_factor*=0.8; fail++
        if fail>50: break? or random restart
```

Let's draft code.

Top-level docstring: "Search for nonnegative vectors minimizing 2n*max(self-conv)/sum^2. Uses softmax L-BFGS optimization in Fourier/softplus representation plus mirror descent and resampling; falls back to valid two-block sequence."

Helper functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog  # may not use
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT=True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT=False
```

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

- check finite.
- p=softmax
- n=p.size

- P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
- c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:m]
- c=np.maximum(c,0)
- y=np.clip(n*c, 0, 100)
- S=np.sum(y**q)
- if S<=0: J=n? etc
- J=(S/m)**(1/q)
- if q==1: Dy=y/J maybe? For q=1 not use. For q>1: D=J**(1-q) * np.power(y, q-1) / m. For y=0 and q-1>0, D=0.
- rev=np.zeros(L); rev[:m]=D[::-1]
- R=np.fft.rfft(rev,L)
- ch=np.fft.irfft(P*R,L)
- h=ch[m-1 - np.arange(n)]
- h=np.maximum? D nonnegative so h nonnegative; tiny negative clip.
- Gp=2*n*h
- dot=np.dot(p,Gp)
- grad=p*(Gp-dot)
- return p,J,grad
Need q maybe float. For q=2..16.
Normalization of D? D sum maybe not 1. That's okay; objective scale consistent. If D has large values? For y ~1, J~1, D~1/m, sum~1. Good.

```
def _c_and_h(p,w):
  n=p.size; m=2*n-1; L=_next_pow2(max(2*n-1, n+m-1))
  P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
  c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:m]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
  rev=np.zeros(L); rev[:m]=w[::-1]
  R=np.fft.rfft(rev,L)
  ch=np.fft.irfft(P*R,L)
  h=ch[m-1 - np.arange(n)]
  h=np.maximum(h,0)
  return c,h
```
But in each iter w depends on c. We'll do.
- current_score = 2*c.max().
- Best update.

```
def _mirror_update(p, h, gamma, product=False):
   val = p*h if product else h
   center = float(np.dot(p, val))  # probability-weighted mean
   z = -gamma*(val - center)
   z = np.clip(z, -5,5)
   p2 = p * np.exp(z)
   p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-15)
   p2/=p2.sum()
   return p2
```

```
    # Sort inits? no.

    per_start = min(120, remaining / len(inits)) but at least 20.
```

```
   if not finite score: return
```

```
def _optimize_n(n, time_deadline, rng, candidates):
    p0 = _two_block_p(n)
    score0 = _score_fft(p0)
    add candidate
    local_best_p = p0.copy(); local_best_score=score0
    # Additional initial g: from local_best, and modes.
    inits=[]
    inits.append(_g_from_p(local_best_p))
    for mode in range(10): inits.append(_make_init_g(n, mode, rng))
    # Update with random resampled from global best if available? propose passes maybe.
    remaining = time_deadline - now
    per = min(60.0, remaining / max(1,len(inits)))
    # Always run two best promising inits with q8 and q16
    # Use rank? no.
    count=0
    for g0 in inits:
       if now > time_deadline - 10: break
       stage_dead = now + min(45.0, remaining/ max(1,len(inits)-count))
       g = _run_lbfgs(g0, n, 8, stage_dead, maxfun=800)
       p = _softmax(g); score = _score_fft(p)
       if score < local_best_score: update; add candidate
       # if score < 1.7, polish q16 for min(20s,1/4 remaining)
       if score < 1.7 and now < time_deadline-20:
           g2 = _run_lbfgs(g, n, 16, now+min(20.0, (time_deadline-now)*0.25), maxfun=500)
           p2=...
           update
       count++
       # If local best < 1.52 and no time, break?
    # Mirror refine local_best for remaining maybe up to 120s
    if now + 5 < time_deadline:
       p = _mirror_refine(local_best_p, time_deadline - 10, max_iter=3000, rng=rng)
       score=...
       update/add
    return local_best_p, local_best_score
```

`_score_fft(p)`: compute max c and return 2nmax. Similar to `_convolve_fft`.
```
def _score_fft(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=p.size
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return inf
   p=p/s
   c=_convolve_fft(p)
   m=float(c.max())
   return float(2.0*n*m)
```

Let's write `_resample_p`:
```
def _resample_p(p, m, n):
    p = np.asarray(p,float)
    s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    p=p/s
    if m==n: return p.copy()
    if m==1:
       return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    g=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    g_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, g)
    return _softmax_g(g_new)
```
`_softmax_g` same as `_softmax`.

`_softmax` takes g floats, shifts. It doesn't enforce bounds. For L-BFGS g within bounds. For resampling maybe g large? Clip to [-10,10].

`_g_from_p`:
```
g=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); g -= np.mean(g); g=np.clip(g,-8,8); return g
```

One possible issue: L-BFGS-B with many parameters and gradient with null direction can be slow. To reduce null, we can fix mean of g = 0 during optimization by removing mean before passing to objective/gradient? L-BFGS state can change mean due to numerical; but gradient zero sum means optimizer shouldn't move mean much. However bounds on g absolute with mean not fixed might still create degeneracy. We can project g to zero mean inside `_smooth_compute` by `g = g - np.mean(g)` before softmax. Is that okay? If we modify g, the gradient returned is w.r.t original g? If we subtract mean internally, objective as function of g is actually constant along mean, but if we project, the effective function is not smooth at projection? But since gradient sum zero, subtracting mean has no first-order effect; higher-order okay. It keeps values within bounds maybe? Actually if a coordinate at bound and we subtract mean, might violate bounds? L-BFGS expects function consistent with x; but we can average? Simpler: don't project; keep g as is. Starting mean 0, gradient zero sum, L-BFGS has null direction; may be okay.

Pseudo:
```
def _mirror_refine(p, deadline, max_iter=2000, rng=None, start_power=8):
  p=np.asarray(p,float); if rng None rng=default
  n=p.size; if n==0 return p
  p=_norm(p)
  best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fft(p)
  current=best.copy(); score=best_score
  gamma_factor=1.0
  fail=0
  powers=[4,8,16,32,64,32,16,8]
  it=0
  while it<max_iter and time.monotonic()<deadline-5:
     c,h? Need w first. Compute c = _convolve_fft(current); score=2n max; update best.
     m=2n-1; maxc = c.max()
     if maxc<=0: break
     power = powers[it % len(powers)]
     w=np.maximum(c,0)**power
     sw=w.sum(); if sw>0: w=w/sw; else uniform
     # mix uniform
     w = 0.95*w + 0.05*np.ones(m)/m
     h = _corr_w_p(current,w)
     if h not finite break
     scale = float(np.std(h)) + 1e-12
     gamma = (0.5 / scale) * gamma_factor
     p1 = _mirror_update(current,h,gamma,product=False)
     s1 = _score_fft(p1)
     if s1 < score - 1e-12:
        current=p1; score=s1; gamma_factor=min(1.5, gamma_factor*1.03); fail=0
     else:
        # try product
        val = current*h
        scale2 = float(np.std(val)) + 1e-12
        gamma2 = (0.5 / scale2) * gamma_factor
        p2 = _mirror_update(current,val,gamma2,product=False?) val already product; use `_mirror_update(p,val,gamma2)` (product flag unnecessary)
        s2=_score_fft(p2)
        if s2 < score -1e-12:
           current=p2; score=s2; fail=0
        else:
           # block perturb
           p3=_block_perturb(current,rng)
           s3=_score_fft(p3)
           if s3 < score-1e-12:
              current=p3; score=s3; gamma_factor=1.0; fail=0
           else:
              gamma_factor *= 0.75
              fail +=1
              if fail > 80: break
     if score < best_score:
        best=current.copy(); best_score=score
     it+=1
  return best
```
But `_mirror_update(p, val, gamma)` centers val by dot(p,val). For h, val scale ~1/n; for product, val scale ~1/n^2. Both with gamma=0.5/scale. Good. We don't need product flag.
Define:
```
def _mirror_update(p, val, gamma):
  center = float(np.dot(p, val))
  z = -gamma*(val-center)
  np.clip(z,-6,6,out=z?)
  p2=p*np.exp(z)
  p2=np.maximum(p2,1e-18); p2/=p2.sum(); return p2
```
If val has large values, clip.

```
def _block_perturb(p, rng):

  start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
```

```
def _run_lbfgs(g0, n, q, stage_deadline, maxfun_hint=1500):
   if not HAVE_MINIMIZER: return g0
   g0 = np.array(g0, dtype=float)
   g0 = g0 - np.mean(g0)
   g0 = np.clip(g0, -8.0, 8.0)
   maxfun = int(maxfun_hint)
   if maxfun < 50: return g0
   options = {'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxiter': max(2, maxfun//2), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':8}
   try:
      res = minimize(_smooth_objective, g0, jac=_smooth_gradient, args=(q,n,stage_deadline), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-8.0,8.0)]*n, options=options)
      if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
          g=np.array(res.x,float)
      else:
          g=g0
   except Exception:
      last=_LAST_G[0]
      if last is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(last)):
         g=np.array(last,float)
      else:
         g=g0
   g = g - np.mean(g)
   g = np.clip(g, -8.0, 8.0)
   return g
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(a convolved with a) / (sum(a)^2)

The search uses:
  * softmax-parameterized sequences,
  * L-BFGS optimization of a smooth Lq surrogate for the maximum coefficient,
  * mirror-descent/critical-point refinement,
  * random block perturbations,
  * resampling / expansion of good small-scale solutions,
  * exact final verification with evaluate_sequence when time allows.

A valid two-block fallback is always available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_MINIMIZER = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_MINIMIZER = False


class _Abort(Exception):
    """Raised inside objective functions to stop time-limited optimization."""
    pass


_LAST_G = [None]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _check_time(deadline):
    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        raise _Abort("time expired")


def _norm_nonnegative(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(p, dtype=float)
    return p / s


def _softmax_g(g):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=8.0, neginf=-8.0)
    g = np.clip(g, -10.0, 10.0)
    g = g - float(np.max(g))
    p = np.exp(g)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p0 = np.ones_like(g, dtype=float) / float(g.size)
        return p0
    return p / s


def _g_from_p(p):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    g = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    g = np.clip(g, -8.0, 8.0)
    return g


def _convolve_fft(p):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _score_fft(p):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = _convolve_fft(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))


def _corr_w_p(w, p, L=None):
    """
    h_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, for i=0..n-1.
    w has length 2n-1.
    """
    n = int(p.size)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = _next_pow2(max(2 * n - 1, n + m - 1))
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    rev = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    rev[:m] = w[::-1]
    g = np.fft.rfft(rev, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(f * g, L)
    idx = m - 1 - np.arange(n)
    h = h[idx]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(h, 0.0)


def _smooth_compute(g, q, n):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=8.0, neginf=-8.0)
    g = np.clip(g, -10.0, 10.0)

    p = _softmax_g(g)
    p = p[:n]
    if p.size != n:
        tmp = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        k = min(n, p.size)
        tmp[:k] = p[:k]
        p = _norm_nonnegative(tmp)

    n = int(p.size)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(max(2 * n - 1, n + m - 1))

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)

    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    # y_k = n c_k, so final score is 2 * max(y).
    y = n * c
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 100.0)

    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        yq = y ** float(q)
    S = float(np.sum(yq))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        # Should not happen for a normalized positive vector, but be safe.
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        return p, 1e6, grad

    J = float((S / float(m)) ** (1.0 / float(q)))
    if not np.isfinite(J) or J <= 0.0:
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        return p, 1e6, grad

    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        D = (J ** (1.0 - float(q))) * (y ** (float(q) - 1.0)) / float(m)
    D = np.where(np.isfinite(D), D, 0.0)

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    rev = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    rev[:m] = D[::-1]
    fd = np.fft.rfft(rev, L)
    ch = np.fft.irfft(fp * fd, L)
    idx = m - 1 - np.arange(n)
    h = ch[idx]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)

    Gp = 2.0 * float(n) * h
    dot = float(np.dot(p, Gp))
    grad = p * (Gp - dot)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    return p, J, grad


def _smooth_objective(g, q, n, deadline):
    _check_time(deadline)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    _LAST_G[0] = g.copy()
    _, J, _ = _smooth_compute(g, q, n)
    if not np.isfinite(J):
        return 1e100
    return float(J)


def _smooth_gradient(g, q, n, deadline):
    _check_time(deadline)
    _, _, grad = _smooth_compute(g, q, n)
    return grad


def _run_lbfgs(g0, n, q, stage_deadline, maxfun_hint=1200):
    g0 = np.asarray(g0, dtype=float).copy()
    if g0.size != n:
        return g0

    if not HAVE_MINIMIZER:
        return g0

    if stage_deadline is None or time.monotonic() >= float(stage_deadline):
        return g0

    g0 = np.nan_to_num(g0, nan=0.0, posinf=8.0, neginf=-8.0)
    g0 = np.clip(g0, -8.0, 8.0)
    g0 = g0 - float(np.mean(g0))
    g0 = np.clip(g0, -8.0, 8.0)

    maxfun = int(max(50, maxfun_hint))
    options = {
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxiter": max(2, maxfun // 2),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxls": 8,
    }
    bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * int(n)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _smooth_objective,
            g0,
            jac=_smooth_gradient,
            args=(q, n, stage_deadline),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        else:
            g = g0
    except Exception:
        last = _LAST_G[0]
        g = g0
        if last is not None:
            last = np.asarray(last, dtype=float)
            if last.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(last)):
                # Accept a trial point only if it is at least as good as start.
                p_start = _softmax_g(g0)
                p_last = _softmax_g(last)
                s_start = _score_fft(p_start)
                s_last = _score_fft(p_last)
                if np.isfinite(s_last) and s_last <= s_start + 1e-12:
                    g = last

    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return g0
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    g = np.clip(g, -8.0, 8.0)
    return g


def _init_g(n, mode, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, n))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    mode = int(mode)

    if mode == 0:
        g = rng.normal(0.0, 0.20, size=n)
    elif mode == 1:
        g = 0.65 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
    elif mode == 2:
        g = -0.65 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
    elif mode == 3:
        g = (
            0.55 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            - 0.22 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
            + 0.10 * np.cos(5.0 * np.pi * x)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
        )
    elif mode == 4:
        g = (
            -0.55 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            + 0.22 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
            - 0.10 * np.cos(5.0 * np.pi * x)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
        )
    elif mode == 5:
        g = (
            0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            - 0.35 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
            - 0.08 * np.cos(5.0 * np.pi * x)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
        )
    elif mode == 6:
        g = (
            -0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            + 0.35 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
            + 0.08 * np.cos(5.0 * np.pi * x)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
        )
    elif mode == 7:
        g = 0.85 * np.tanh(7.0 * (x - 0.5)) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=n)
    elif mode == 8:
        g = -0.85 * np.tanh(7.0 * (x - 0.5)) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=n)
    elif mode == 9:
        g = 0.50 * np.tanh(12.0 * (x - 0.35)) + 0.35 * rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
    elif mode == 10:
        for k in (1, 3, 5, 7):
            g += rng.normal(0.0, 0.18) * np.cos(float(k) * np.pi * x)
        g += rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
    elif mode == 11:
        for k in range(1, 7):
            g += rng.normal(0.0, 0.12) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x)
            g += rng.normal(0.0, 0.12) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x)
    else:
        # Endpoint-heavy but anti-symmetric-ish mixture.
        g = (
            0.30 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            + 0.25 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
            + 0.15 * np.sin(np.pi * x)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
        )

    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=8.0, neginf=-8.0)
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    g = np.clip(g, -8.0, 8.0)
    return g


def _two_block_g(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, n))
    # Smooth version of the optimal two-block construction.
    g = 0.85 * np.tanh(10.0 * (x - 0.5))
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return np.clip(g, -8.0, 8.0)


def _block_perturb(p, rng):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p.copy()

    max_w = max(2, n // 4)
    min_w = 1
    if max_w <= min_w:
        w = 1
    else:
        w = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w + 1))
    start = 0
    if n - w >= 1:
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

    q = p.copy()
    fac = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=w))
    q[start:start + w] *= fac
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-15)
    return _norm_nonnegative(q)


def _resample_p(p, n_new):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    n_old = int(p.size)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return p.copy()
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, 1.0 / max(1, n_new), dtype=float)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    g = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    g = np.interp(x_new, x_old, g)
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return _softmax_g(g)


def _safe_eval(seq):
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if np.isfinite(val):
        return val
    return float("inf")


def _add_candidate(cand_list, p, score, cap=16):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _score_fft(p)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return

    if len(cand_list) < cap:
        cand_list.append((p.copy(), float(score)))
        return

    # Replace worst if this is better than worst.
    worst_i = 0
    worst_s = cand_list[0][1]
    for i in range(1, len(cand_list)):
        if cand_list[i][1] > worst_s:
            worst_s = cand_list[i][1]
            worst_i = i

    if score < worst_s - 1e-12:
        cand_list[worst_i] = (p.copy(), float(score))


def _mirror_update(p, val, gamma):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    val = np.asarray(val, dtype=float)
    if val.size != p.size:
        return p.copy()
    val = np.nan_to_num(val, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    center = float(np.dot(p, val))
    z = -float(gamma) * (val - center)
    z = np.clip(z, -6.0, 6.0)
    q = p * np.exp(z)
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-18)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    return q / s


def _c_single(w, p):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(max(2 * n - 1, n + m - 1))
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    rev = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    rev[:m] = w[::-1]
    fd = np.fft.rfft(rev, L)
    ch = np.fft.irfft(fp * fd, L)
    idx = m - 1 - np.arange(n)
    h = ch[idx]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return c, h


def _mirror_refine(p, deadline, rng, max_iter=1200):
    p = _norm_nonnegative(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(p)
    cur = p.copy()
    cur_score = best_score
    gamma_factor = 1.0
    fails = 0
    powers = (4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 32, 16, 8)

    it = 0
    while it < max_iter and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 5.0:
        c = _convolve_fft(cur)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            break

        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = cur.copy()

        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        m = c.size
        pw = float(powers[it % len(powers)])
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            w = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), pw)
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
        else:
            w = w / sw
            # Slight uniform mixing improves robustness.
            w = 0.95 * w + 0.05 * (1.0 / float(m))
            s2 = float(w.sum())
            if s2 > 0.0:
                w = w / s2

        _, h = _c_single(w, cur)
        if h.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            break

        scale = float(np.std(h)) + 1e-14
        gamma = (0.55 / scale) * gamma_factor

        cand = _mirror_update(cur, h, gamma)
        s_cand = _score_fft(cand)

        if np.isfinite(s_cand) and s_cand < cur_score - 1e-13:
            cur = cand
            cur_score = s_cand
            gamma_factor = min(1.6, gamma_factor * 1.05)
            fails = 0
        else:
            # Product-contribution style update.
            val = cur * h
            if val.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(val)):
                scale2 = float(np.std(val)) + 1e-14
                gamma2 = (0.55 / scale2) * gamma_factor
                cand2 = _mirror_update(cur, val, gamma2)
                s_cand2 = _score_fft(cand2)
                if np.isfinite(s_cand2) and s_cand2 < cur_score - 1e-13:
                    cur = cand2
                    cur_score = s_cand2
                    gamma_factor = min(1.6, gamma_factor * 1.03)
                    fails = 0
                else:
                    cand3 = _block_perturb(cur, rng)
                    s_cand3 = _score_fft(cand3)
                    if np.isfinite(s_cand3) and s_cand3 < cur_score - 1e-13:
                        cur = cand3
                        cur_score = s_cand3
                        gamma_factor = 1.0
                        fails = 0
                    else:
                        gamma_factor *= 0.75
                        fails += 1
                        if fails > 90:
                            break
            else:
                gamma_factor *= 0.75
                fails += 1
                if fails > 90:
                    break

        it += 1

    return best_p


def _optimize_size(n, stage_deadline, rng, seed_p=None):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    stage_deadline = float(stage_deadline)

    # A strong nontrivial starting point.
    g2b = _two_block_g(n)
    p_best = _softmax_g(g2b)
    p_best = _norm_nonnegative(p_best)
    s_best = _score_fft(p_best)
    print(f"    size {n}: two-block start score={s_best:.8f}", flush=True)

    inits = [g2b]

    if seed_p is not None:
        p_seed = _resample_p(seed_p, n)
        g_seed = _g_from_p(p_seed)
        s_seed = _score_fft(p_seed)
        inits.append(g_seed)
        if np.isfinite(s_seed) and s_seed < s_best:
            p_best = p_seed.copy()
            s_best = s_seed

    modes = 0
    for mode in range(12):
        inits.append(_init_g(n, mode, rng))
        modes += 1

    # Limit number of L-BFGS starts by remaining time.
    elapsed = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, stage_deadline - elapsed - 45.0)
    if remaining > 25.0:
        per_start = min(75.0, remaining / max(1.0, float(len(inits))))
        per_start = max(12.0, per_start)

        polished = 0
        max_polish = 4
        for idx, g0 in enumerate(inits):
            if time.monotonic() > stage_deadline - 45.0:
                break
            if per_start <= 8.0:
                break

            sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + min(per_start, stage_deadline - 30.0)
            g = _run_lbfgs(g0, n, 8.0, sub_deadline, maxfun_hint=900)
            p = _softmax_g(g)
            sc = _score_fft(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                print(f"    size {n}: init {idx} Lq-8 score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)
                if sc < s_best:
                    p_best = p.copy()
                    s_best = sc

            # Polish promising points with a sharper surrogate.
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 1.78 and polished < max_polish and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 60.0:
                polish_dead = time.monotonic() + min(35.0, max(10.0, stage_deadline - 60.0 - time.monotonic() + 1.0))
                g2 = _run_lbfgs(g, n, 16.0, polish_dead, maxfun_hint=650)
                p2 = _softmax_g(g2)
                sc2 = _score_fft(p2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2):
                    print(f"      size {n}: init {idx} Lq-16 score={sc2:.8f}", flush=True)
                    if sc2 < s_best:
                        p_best = p2.copy()
                        s_best = sc2
                polished += 1

    if time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 25.0:
        mirror_deadline = min(stage_deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.35 * (stage_deadline - time.monotonic())))
        p_m = _mirror_refine(p_best, mirror_deadline, rng, max_iter=1500)
        sc_m = _score_fft(p_m)
        if np.isfinite(sc_m) and sc_m < s_best:
            p_best = p_m.copy()
            s_best = sc_m
            print(f"    size {n}: mirror score={s_best:.8f}", flush=True)

    return p_best, s_best


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0, **kwargs):
    """Search for a low-evaluation non-negative sequence under the time budget."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = 1800.0
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 10.0:
        budget = 1800.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 10.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20250924)

    # Optional parent seed.
    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            ppar = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
            ppar = _norm_nonnegative(ppar)
            if ppar.size > 0 and ppar.size <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
                sp = _safe_eval([float(v) for v in ppar])
                print(f"parent exact score={sp:.8f}", flush=True)
                if np.isfinite(sp) and sp < 2.05:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            ppar = None

    candidates = []

    # Robust fallback.
    fb_n = 1024
    fb_p = _softmax_g(_two_block_g(fb_n))
    _add_candidate(candidates, fb_p, _score_fft(fb_p))

    best_global_p = fb_p.copy()
    best_global_score = _score_fft(fb_p)

    sizes = [
        (256, 60.0),
        (512, 180.0),
        (1024, 650.0),
        (2048, 700.0),
        (4096, 500.0),
    ]

    for n, alloc in sizes:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        alloc = min(alloc, rem)
        if alloc < 60.0:
            break

        stage_dead = time.monotonic() + alloc
        print(f"optimizing size={n}, alloc={alloc:.1f}s", flush=True)
        p, sc = _optimize_size(int(n), stage_dead, rng, seed_p=best_global_p)
        if p is not None:
            _add_candidate(candidates, p, sc)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_score:
                best_global_score = sc
                best_global_p = p.copy()
                print(f"new global best score={best_global_score:.8f} at n={n}", flush=True)

    # Expansion / resampling of the best smooth solution can improve finite-n accuracy.
    if best_global_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        m0 = int(best_global_p.size)
        for target_n in (2048, 4096, 8192):
            if target_n == m0:
                continue
            if target_n > 12000:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 70.0:
                break
            p_x = _resample_p(best_global_p, target_n)
            sc_x = _score_fft(p_x)
            if np.isfinite(sc_x):
                print(f"expanded n={target_n} score={sc_x:.8f}", flush=True)
                _add_candidate(candidates, p_x, sc_x)
                if sc_x < best_global_score:
                    best_global_score = sc_x
                    best_global_p = p_x.copy()

            short_mirror = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
            if time.monotonic() < short_mirror - 10.0:
                p_m = _mirror_refine(p_x, short_mirror, rng, max_iter=500)
                sc_m = _score_fft(p_m)
                if np.isfinite(sc_m):
                    print(f"expanded mirror n={target_n} score={sc_m:.8f}", flush=True)
                    _add_candidate(candidates, p_m, sc_m)
                    if sc_m < best_global_score:
                        best_global_score = sc_m
                        best_global_p = p_m.copy()

                best_global_p = p_m

    # Final exact evaluation of a few best surrogate-score candidates.
    exact_best_p = best_global_p
    exact_best_score = _safe_eval([float(v) for v in best_global_p])
    print(f"final top exact score before extras={exact_best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    if candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        top = []
        for _ in range(5):
            bi = -1
            bv = float("inf")
            for i in range(len(candidates)):
                if i in top:
                    continue
                if candidates[i][1] < bv:
                    bv = candidates[i][1]
                    bi = i
            if bi < 0:
                break
            top.append(bi)

        for idx in top:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            p = candidates[idx][1] if False else candidates[idx][0]
            if p.size > 12000:
                continue
            seq = [float(v) for v in p]
            sc = _safe_eval(seq)
            print(f"exact candidate idx={idx} n={p.size} score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and (not np.isfinite(exact_best_score) or sc < exact_best_score):
                exact_best_score = sc
                exact_best_p = p

    if exact_best_p is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(exact_best_p)):
        exact_best_p = fb_p
        exact_best_score = _safe_eval([float(v) for v in exact_best_p])

    final = np.asarray(exact_best_p, dtype=float)
    final = _norm_nonnegative(final)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)

    print(f"FINAL returned n={final.size} approximate exact score={exact_best_score:.8f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
    return [float(v) for v in final]
```
